I’ve missed things I meant to do, like calling the doctor’s office back, or picking up a prescription. And sometimes I cut it too close leaving for my cousin’s appointment. It’s not constant, but it’s been happening.\n\nDoctor: Have these memory or attention problems been getting in the way of your daily activities, like managing your work or family responsibilities?\n\nPatient: Some, yeah. It’s not… ruining everything, but it’s caused some friction at home. My wife gets frustrated, like she can’t quite count on me with my cousin’s care. And with the catering stuff, I’ve had to go back and double-check or redo things so little jobs don’t get mixed up.\n\nDoctor: Over the past two weeks, have you felt down, hopeless, or lost interest or pleasure in most things?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… not really in a big way. I’ve had off days, sure, but I wouldn’t say I’ve been hopeless or not enjoying most things.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any days where it felt like you couldn’t get going, or you felt slowed down physically or mentally, even if you weren’t stressed or busy?\n\nPatient: Not really that I’ve noticed. I mean, I have slower mornings sometimes, but I usually figure I’m just tired or dragging a bit. I wouldn’t say it’s been a clear thing.\n\nDoctor: Do you notice that your mind feels 'foggy' or slowed down, especially when you're trying to think or make decisions?\n\nPatient: I don’t think so. I mean, I get distracted or have to think things through twice sometimes, but “foggy” or slowed down… I wouldn’t really put it that way.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. I mean, I have my off days, but I haven’t noticed anything like that.\n\nDoctor: Do you find that you lose track of conversations or tasks more often than you used to, even when you’re making a real effort to stay focused?\n\nPatient: Yes… I’d say so. It’s not… ruining everything, but it’s caused some friction at home. My wife gets frustrated, like she can’t quite count on me with my cousin’s care. I’ve had off days, sure, but I wouldn’t say I’ve been hopeless or not enjoying most things.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any days where it felt like you couldn’t get going, or you felt slowed down physically or mentally, even if you weren’t stressed or busy?\n\nPatient: Not really that I’ve noticed.
